Replayed on R4's retry after `c132f5c2`, the stale identity (`ATT-CP-1-80f65efd…`, digest `3ae3db0c…`) is only inside the refused answer, and the current one (`ATT-CP-1-90b07483…`, digest `91833185…`) only after it. **Fallbacks** to D30's block, byte for byte as before ("from the beginning"): the body is not a decodable transport envelope (N50), holds text no reader can see (`hides_text`) or will not cross `BoundaryText`; the checks report nothing (then there is no block at all, as before); or the request carrying it would pass `MAX_REQUEST_BYTES`, the transport ceiling `request_size` and `priced_request` both hold a request to (`canonical._sent_prompt`, measured by the provider's own `request_bytes`, `>` the bound as `request_size` refuses); or (fix round 1, M1) the run's remaining ceiling would not pay for the request carrying it. `check_context` decides that from `remaining`, the read `_reserve` refuses on, priced at the price the provider bills at (`_affordable`). `execute_handoff` decides from the attempt's own reservation row, the record of that choice (`_covered`), never from a second read of the ceiling. So the priced and the sent prompt stay one prompt, and a crash after the reservation rebuilds the same one. Before this, a retry the plain request fit but the carrying one did not was refused `BUDGET_CEILING_REACHED`, which left the earlier refusal standing: a retry D30 would have made was lost. **D30's rules kept:** the answer crosses `BoundaryText`; the block renders last and the answer is folded into the section tag beside the checks, so no marker it holds -- even one under the tag of the prompt it answered, the only tag it could know -- can close the sub-section or the block; it goes into that one request only, never a log, refusal or row; a first attempt's bytes are unchanged (an answer handed to a build with no checks changes nothing) and no `prompt` golden moved. G1-16's "never the value" now holds for the checks' lines, which still name fields only; the value is in the model's own answer carried back. **Ledger and replay:** the retry prompt is still a pure function of stored rows and the blob, so a crash between the refusal and the retry changes nothing (`second_attempt_due`); the resumed 3rd attempt carries the 2nd answer, never the 1st. **Invariant 8:** the reservation already prices the request actually sent. `check_context` measures the prompt `_sent_prompt` builds, the answer included, with `request_size`, and the loop reserves `priced_request` of that size. `execute_handoff` rebuilds the same prompt through `_sent_prompt` and `_within_reservation` re-prices it, so a retry priced without the answer and sent with it refuses `RESERVATION_BELOW_REQUEST` before the call (checked by mutation). A retry the ceiling cannot pay for even without the answer still leaves the refusal before it standing (D30, third addendum). **Size**, R4's CP-1 retry after attempt 2 (`c132f5c2`, rebuilt read-only, `scratchpad/rp/r4size.py`): the refused body is 61,852 bytes; the prompt grows from 817,195 to 880,451 bytes (+63,256, the restated front matter included) and the request from 840,296 to 905,068 bytes (+64,772, +7.7%), 3,289,236 bytes under the ceiling. The input cost of that retry grows by the same 7.6%; the completion is a whole answer either way. Host prompt text: `icm/HOST_INTEGRITY_v1.json` and `host_pin.py` regenerated (`scripts/host_manifest.py`). `caos.icm.REPAIR_BLOCK` is declared conditional in all 27 stage contracts, regenerated by `scripts/icm_stages.py --write`; `validator_feedback`'s condition now says it renders when the answer is not carried. Test updates: the two G1-16 tests assert the value is absent from the checks rather than from the whole prompt; `_own` also masks the JSON-escaped invocation digest inside the carried answer; and `tests/test_icm.py` counts both retry blocks. **Evidence:** `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_retry_carries_the_refused_answer_and_asks_for_it_corrected`, `::test_a_retry_after_an_answer_that_is_not_the_transport_carries_none`, `::test_a_retry_too_large_to_carry_its_answer_asks_from_the_beginning`, `::test_the_answer_is_dropped_only_past_the_transport_ceiling`, `::test_a_forged_end_marker_in_the_refused_answer_cannot_close_its_block`, `::test_a_first_attempt_and_an_answerless_retry_are_built_as_before`, `::test_a_retry_carrying_its_answer_reserves_for_the_larger_request`, `::test_carried_answer_is_the_transport_across_the_boundary_or_nothing`, `::test_a_retry_the_ceiling_cannot_pay_to_carry_is_sent_plain` (M1: ceiling = the first reservation plus the midpoint of the two retry reservations; two CP-0 prompts, the second plain), and `::test_the_third_attempt_survives_a_crash_after_the_second_refusal` (now also checks which answer is carried). Each was checked against a mutation: dropping the answer from the tag fold (the forged-marker test now compares the retry's tag with and without the answer, fix round 1, M3), removing the size fallback, pricing the prompt without the answer, carrying a non-transport, carrying nothing, either ceiling decision always carrying (M1), and an empty restated front matter (M2). `_diagnostic`'s docstring now names the retry as the stored body's second reader (M4). N135 records the per-model prompt bound the fallback does not see (M5). Each mutation fails at least one of these tests. **Wording (F496, 2026-10-04):** "keep everything else as it was" did not hold the answer whole: in live run C2 CP-4's repairs shrank from 88,487 to 42,774 bytes, dropping 26 registers. The block's last instruction now reads "Return the complete corrected answer, as one new JSON object: every H2 section, every register table and every row and citation of the refused answer, carried over unchanged except where the checks above name a fault. Fix what the checks name. Do not shorten, summarise, merge or omit any part, and still apply every rule of this request." The markers, the tag fold, the restated front matter and the fallbacks are unchanged; `icm/HOST_INTEGRITY_v1.json` and `host_pin.py` regenerated (`scripts/host_manifest.py`).
- F495 (2026-10-04, live-loop fault L3, run C1 CP-1) — A wrong source_id and a row with dropped cells are each told what to fix. In live run C1 (`czr-2026q2-covenant-refinancing`, `caos_qualify_ee68c335…`) CP-1 was refused three times and the run stopped `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED`; the repair-mode retries (D104) converged from 38 citation faults to 7 to 1. Attempt 1 (`8ea87f66`): 37 of 59 citations named the source_id `a6e3ce6e-a908-4b1b-8e4e-a7cdf2069e23`, which splices the 10-Q's id (`a6e3ce6e-a908-4b81-…`) with the 10-K's suffix (`…-8e4e-a7cdf2069e23`), and were told they "name a page or line this node was not given", which names nothing to fix. Attempt 3 (`18e054c2`): citation 23 quoted "Balance at June 30 | 41 | 92 | 545", the delivered row of page 11 with alternate period columns left out ("Balance at June 30 | 41 | 39 | 92 | 86 | 545 | 586"), and was told only "in no evidence line"; F493's near miss passes over it, its length bound excluding a row that lost three of seven cells. Fix (hints only, under D82's retry feedback; acceptance unchanged, invariant 11, `WHOLE_LINE`): (1) a citation refused `CITATION_NOT_DELIVERED` whose source_id is not one of the request's delivered sources -- the list the final check prints -- gets `LineHint.unknown_source` (`canonical._anchoring_line`), and `handoff.anchoring_line` says, grouped by that id: `citations 1, 3 … of T name source_id X, which is not one of this request's sources; use one of the source_id values listed in the final check`, grouping citations by bad id and holder and adding `, and the lines are in source Y` to a group whose citations each have exactly one delivered source (the same Y) holding the quote as one whole line of its cited page, so one bad id whose citations split between a holder and none gets two clauses (task review, accepted: each is accurate) (`LineHint.held_by`, `canonical._holders`: one dict over the delivered blocks' own text, by page and words joined by one space, built only when such a citation exists -- delivered text only, D82's M2). A valid id on a page or line the node was not given keeps D82's wording. (2) `caos.evidence.citations.cells_line`, beside `near_line` and asked after it (`canonical._near_hint`, now handed the search): a quote holding `CELL_SEPARATOR` (" | ") names the one delivered line of its source with the same first cell whose cells hold the quote's, in order, as a subsequence, with more cells than the quote; `citation N of T leaves out cells of the evidence line of page P that begins "<first 12 words>"; quote the whole row, every cell` (`LineHint.cells`, `handoff._placed`), and `... of page P, not its cited page, ... every cell, and cite page P` when moved. Bounded as F493: the first cell is the prefilter (one pass over the lines), more than `NEAR_MEASURED` (64) rows sharing it, or two candidate rows, give no hint, and each check is linear in cells. Both are placements under `MAX_ANCHORING_CHARS`: the kept list (F491) is dropped first, then placements, these included, from the last citation back, each keeping its number under the rule's wording. C1's three CP-1 answers replayed (`scratchpad/l3/replay.py`): attempt 1 now reads "citations 1, 3, …, 21 and 9 more of 59 name source_id a6e3ce6e-a908-4b1b-8e4e-a7cdf2069e23, which is not one of this request's sources; use one of the source_id values listed in the final check, and the lines are in source a6e3ce6e-a908-4b81-93d8-6276ab056fac", and citations 23, 28, 30-34 and 37 the same clause without a holder (their quotes are not one whole delivered line of the cited page); attempt 2 (`d22d0e23`) tells citations 30-34 the Adjusted EBITDA rows of pages 20-21 they left cells out of, and citation 5 its unknown id and holder (its citation 23, the same row reordered, stays "in no evidence line"); attempt 3 tells citation 23 "leaves out cells of the evidence line of page 11 that begins "Balance at June 30 | 41 | 39 | 92 | 86"; quote the whole row, every cell". The whole anchoring line over 59 citations and 4,958 delivered blocks takes 0.22-0.33 s, as before (0.24-0.36 s). No `prompt` golden carries retry feedback, so none moved. Evidence: `tests/test_whole_line_citations.py::test_cells_line_names_the_one_row_a_quote_left_cells_out_of`, `::test_a_dropped_cells_row_is_hinted_from_delivered_lines_only`, `::test_placing_a_dropped_cells_row_stays_linear_in_the_lines_delivered`, `::test_an_unknown_source_id_is_told_so_and_a_delivered_one_keeps_its_wording`; `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_the_anchoring_line_tells_an_unknown_source_and_a_row_missing_cells`.
- D82 amendment (2026-10-04, owner decision of 4 October 2026: "Allow a third retry (Recommended)", to the question "Should a node get a third guided retry (4 attempts)? … GUIDED_RETRIES 2 → 3. Recorded as an amendment to D82. N129's headroom note stays open for the deployed default.") — A node gets three guided retries, not two. Evidence: in live run C1 CP-1's repair-mode retries (D104) converged from 38 citation faults to 7 to 1 and ran out of attempts (F495). Change: `canonical.GUIDED_RETRIES` is 3, so `_feedback_source` makes the 2nd, 3rd and 4th attempts guided retries, each told of the attempt just before it, and `runtime._with_second_attempt` loops at most `1 + GUIDED_RETRIES` (4) passes. The ledger walk is unchanged: the next attempt is a guided retry only when the latest attempt was refused with a `SECOND_ATTEMPT_CODES` code (`HANDOFF_MALFORMED`, `HANDOFF_INCOMPLETE`, `HANDOFF_IDENTITY_MISMATCH`, `HANDOFF_UNDECLARED_FIELD` or an anchoring refusal) and the node holds at most three such refusals; a crash between a refusal and its retry changes nothing, and a fourth refusal stops the run, an operator's later retry being an ordinary attempt. Budget (invariant 8): each retry is reserved and priced like any other attempt, and a retry the ceiling cannot pay for leaves the refusal before it standing (D30, third addendum). N129's headroom note applies more strongly: the default ceiling's headroom covers one worst-case retry, and a FULL node may now take three, so one needing a second or third fails closed `BUDGET_CEILING_REACHED`, never overspending; N129 stays open for the deployed default. No `prompt` golden carries retry feedback, so none moved; no host prompt block names a count. Tests that counted two retries now count three: `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_fourth_refusal_stops_the_run_with_no_fifth_attempt` (renamed from the third-refusal test), `::test_three_guided_retries_per_node_whichever_codes_refused` (renamed), new `::test_the_ledger_makes_the_fourth_attempt_the_last_guided_retry` (the ledger walk on `NodeAttempt`s alone); `tests/test_runtime.py::test_crash_after_a_billed_refused_answer_records_it_and_stops_without_a_call` (now dies after the 4th bill), `::test_a_retry_skips_a_recorded_refusal_and_makes_one_new_attempt`; `tests/test_canonical_runtime.py`, `test_loop_charges.py`, `test_worker.py`, `test_lite_cp1c_contract.py`, `test_lite_full_credit_screen_route.py`, `test_lite_route_e2e_negative.py` (`test_execution_billing.py` already counts `1 + GUIDED_RETRIES`).
- F496 (2026-10-04, live-loop fault L4, run C2 CP-4) — A repair retry asks for the whole answer back, and a quote that runs past the end of its line is told so. In live run C2 (`czr-2026q2-covenant-refinancing`, `caos_qualify_b5549d83…`) CP-4 was refused four times and the run stopped `HANDOFF_MALFORMED`. (1) Shrinking repairs: attempt 1 (`89a63609`) was a complete 88,487-byte answer with 21 citations, refused only for citation faults; its 98,583-byte body was carried into attempt 2 (a 2,561,467-byte request, under `MAX_REQUEST_BYTES` and affordable), which returned 42,774 bytes and dropped 26 registers (T4C.2-T4F.6); attempt 3 grew back to 65,740 bytes with one empty critical cell; attempt 4 shrank to 44,147 bytes, keeping only the "Audit Summary" and "Analysis" H2s, so all 28 citations lost their Evidence Trace quotes. `icm/shared/prompt/validator_repair.md`'s "keep everything else as it was" is replaced by an explicit whole-answer instruction (D104's wording addendum); the D30/D104 structure is unchanged. (2) Citation 16 got the same F493 near-miss hint on all four attempts and was never fixed: its evidence line on page 23 ends mid-sentence at "…required by such Security", a page break splitting the sentence, and the model always quoted on to "…Documents to perfect the Liens created thereunder."; the near-miss hint showed only the line's first 12 words. Fix (a hint only, under D82's retry feedback; acceptance unchanged, invariant 11): `caos.evidence.citations.overrun_line` names the one delivered line of the citation's source that is a strict prefix of the quote, word for word as anchoring reads them (NFC, the line's first and last words standing for the quote's there less edge punctuation, `_edge_equal`), the rest of the quote non-empty. A candidate holds the quote's first `NEAR_WORDS` (6) words, so a short heading is never one; more than `NEAR_MEASURED` (64) candidates, or two overrun lines, give no hint; one pass over the lines and at most 64 linear prefix checks. `canonical._line_hint` asks it before the near miss and the dropped cells (`_near_hint(..., overrun=True)`), so a unique overrun beats a near miss. The clause (`LineHint.ends`, the line's last `handoff.END_WORDS` (8) words, `handoff._placed`): `citation N of T runs past the end of the evidence line of page P, which ends "<last 8 words>"; quote that line only, ending where it ends (text after it is a separate evidence line)`, and `... of page P, not its cited page, which ends "..."; quote that line only, ending where it ends, and cite page P (...)` when moved. It is a placement like any other under `MAX_ANCHORING_CHARS` (F491's drop order). Only delivered text is compared, so an undelivered page is never read (D82's M2). C2's four CP-4 answers replayed (`scratchpad/l3/replay4.py`): citation 16 is told on every attempt "runs past the end of the evidence line of page 23, which ends "and take all actions required by such Security"; quote that line only, ending where it ends (text after it is a separate evidence line)"; attempt 1's citations 9, 15 and 21, before told only the rule's wording, are told the same of pages 11, 28 (not its cited page, "and cite page 28") and 20. The whole anchoring line over 21-28 citations and 7,749 delivered blocks takes 0.42-0.49 s, as before (0.40-0.50 s). No `prompt` golden carries retry feedback, so none moved. Evidence: `tests/test_whole_line_citations.py::test_overrun_line_names_the_one_line_a_quote_runs_past`, `::test_an_overrun_is_hinted_before_a_near_miss_from_delivered_lines_only`, `::test_placing_an_overrun_stays_linear_in_the_lines_delivered`; `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_the_anchoring_line_tells_a_quote_that_runs_past_its_line` and `::test_a_retry_carries_the_refused_answer_and_asks_for_it_corrected` (the block's new wording, `REPAIR`).
- F497 (2026-10-04, live-loop fault L5, run C3; F494's producer-guard pattern) — CP-0's T8 demand cells are checked at CP-0's acceptance, where a guided retry can fix them. In live run C3 (`czr-2026q2-covenant-refinancing`, `caos_qualify_55735e3f…`) CP-0, CP-1, CP-4, CP-2 and CP-2D were accepted (CP-4 on attempt 2, after F496), and the run stopped `EVIDENCE_DEMAND_UNRESOLVED` before CP-3C's first attempt: CP-0's accepted T8 "Source files to attach" cells for CP-3C and CP-5 each held 8 items, seven naming pinned members and one, "CZR_2026_Merger_8K.txt", a misspelling of the member "CZR_2026_Merger_Agreement_8K.txt". `selection.select_sources` refuses that half-readable cell, correctly (§95), but with CP-0 accepted nothing could repair it: the discharge was a whole successor run. Fix: `select_sources`' rule now lives in `selection._resolved` (with `_placed` for one item), which returns its refusal as the first item at fault and why (`DemandFault`, `Fault.UNKNOWN`/`AMBIGUOUS`/`PAGES`/`PAGE_FORM`); `select_sources` raises `EVIDENCE_DEMAND_UNRESOLVED` on any, unchanged, and `demand_fault` returns it -- one rule, not a copy. Split so, `select_sources` (17, a baselined legacy function) and `_resolved` both fall under 15, and its `tests/gate_baseline.json` entry is removed (`complexity_baselined` 17 to 16). `canonical._answer`, the one post-call verdict the live call and `replay_billed` share, refuses the gate's answer `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` (in `SECOND_ATTEMPT_CODES`, so the next attempt is a guided retry) after the F494 size check when any route consumer's cell would be refused (`_demand_faults`: `demand_cells` through the vendor parser, `demand_fault` against the run's pinned members, and each member's last page from the gate's delivered blocks, which `gate_view` keeps for every page, so they are the consumer's). A cell that names nothing pinned (`WHOLE_UNMAPPED`, delivered whole) or is empty is accepted as before, and a row for a module not on the route is not checked (it cannot wedge the run). The retry carries one host line per row at fault (`_demand_lines`), the module and the item -- the gate's own text told back, as D30 allows -- across `BoundaryText` and bounded like a vendor message (`handoff._bounded`): `host demand check: T8 row CP-3C names "CZR_2026_Merger_8K.txt", which is no source of this run; write each source by its exact filename as listed in the host source preparation metadata`; an item two members answer to: `... which names more than one source of this run; write each source by its exact filename as listed in the host source preparation metadata`; a page range its member cannot carry: `... whose pages that source does not carry; name pages from 1 to its last page, or the source whole`; and (task review) an unmapped item that begins with a member's exact filename -- by `_matching`, the test `_named` uses, never a looser one -- followed by a page phrase rule 5's form does not read (`selection._unmapped_fault`, `Fault.PAGE_FORM`): `... whose page form the host cannot read; write one range per item, as `<filename> pages <first>-<last>` or `<filename> page <n>`, separated by ";"`, where it was told "no source of this run", which misled. Only the wording moves: such an item is still unmapped, so beside a readable item the gate still refuses `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` and a consumer `EVIDENCE_DEMAND_UNRESOLVED`, and alone the cell is still delivered whole. No item text reaches a refusal, row or log: the refusal is the code alone. The consumer's refusal stays as the backstop for a gate record accepted before this change. Pure over the pin and the answer, so the live call and `replay_billed` give one verdict. Builds: no bundle, prompt block or adapter version moves, so a run pinned to an older build is treated as F494 left it -- its accepted gate record is never re-checked (accepted records are verified by `verify_accepted`, not `_answer`), and only a CP-0 attempt answered from now on is held to the check. Stored answers (`scratchpad/l5/replay_cp0.py`, every CP-0 answer of the 67 `caos_qualify_*` databases on the dev run server, 39 answers): one accepted answer would now be refused -- C3's, at its rows CP-3C and CP-5, both naming "CZR_2026_Merger_8K.txt", the cell that wedged its run; two answers already refused `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` would also have faulted (`a82a07ad…` attempt 3, "CZR_FY2025_10K.txt pages 14-15 and 26-27", a two-range item the page form does not read, for CP-2, CP-2D and CP-3C, now `PAGE_FORM`; `b2942c49…` attempt 3, "MGM_Q2_2026_Earnings_Release.txt pages 1-6", past that member's captured pages, for CP-1C and CP-L10). Evidence: `tests/test_evidence_selection.py::test_demand_fault_names_the_item_select_sources_refuses` (C3's members and cell), `::test_a_page_phrase_the_form_cannot_read_is_told_as_such` (a82a07ad's item shape), `::test_a_demand_the_host_half_reads_is_refused_before_any_attempt` (now the backstop, its gate accepted with the check stubbed out); `tests/test_page_selection.py::test_a_page_range_past_the_source_refuses_the_gate_as_a_retry`, `::test_a_page_range_past_the_source_refuses_the_consumer_before_any_attempt` (the backstop, likewise); `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_misspelt_t8_source_refuses_the_gate_and_its_retry_is_told`, `::test_a_t8_page_phrase_the_host_cannot_read_is_told_its_form`, `::test_a_t8_cell_the_host_reads_whole_or_named_is_accepted_at_the_gate`.
- F498 (2026-10-04, owner decision of 4 October: "Split legal docs by clause (Recommended)", live run C4 CP-4) — The CZR legal instruments are cut at clause boundaries. In live run C4 (`czr-2026q2-covenant-refinancing`) CP-4 was refused four times `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` on legal-document citations: one credit-agreement clause sits on a 1,826- and a 957-character line either side of a page number, and the model alternated between overrunning the first and quoting a fragment of it. The owner's ruling, verbatim: "Re-convert only the credit agreements, amendments, indenture and merger agreement. A line over ~400 characters is split at clause boundaries: "; " before (a)/(i)/(1), "; and", "; provided". Then re-admit the affected sets and restate any key that moved, as in the sentence split. Filings and releases stay as they are." The controller's reading of the ruling (4 October): the option's list ("; " before (a)/(i)/(1), "; and", "; provided") names the clause boundaries by example; an enumerated clause introduced by a comma is the same boundary, and without it the split misses its purpose, since C4's 1,826-character line, whose `(a)`, `(b)`, `(i)` and `(ii)` follow commas, would stay whole. Action: a v4 staging converter (`scratchpad/t6c/convert4.py` and `clauses.py`, outside the repo; v3 in `scratchpad/edgar/` untouched) runs v3 (F492) and then, in the 2020 credit agreement, the Fourth and Fifth Amendments, the 6.50% 2032 notes indenture and the merger 8-K only, cuts every prose sentence over 400 characters at a clause boundary: `; ` or `, ` before an enumerator (`(a)`, `(aa)`, `(i)`, `(xiv)`, `(1)`, `(A)`, `(II)`, optionally followed by a second label); `; and` / `; or` and `, and` / `, or` / `, and/or` before an enumerator (the cut after the conjunction, so the left piece reads `…; and` or `…, and`); `; provided` in any form and `, provided that` / `, provided, however,` (the cut before `provided`); and `:` before an enumerator. A comma-led enumerator that is a cross-reference is not a boundary: the word before the comma is a bare label (`clauses (i), (ii)`), or the enumerator carries punctuation (`(f),`) or is followed by a label, a conjunction or a reference word (`Sections 6.05(p), (q) and (r)`, `(iii) above`). A boundary inside parentheses counts only when that parenthetical is itself enumerated. It is recursive: a piece over 400 characters is cut at all of its boundaries of the shallowest bracket depth present, and each piece still over 400 is cut the same way, until every piece is 400 or less or has no boundary left; a sentence of 400 or less and a table row are never cut, and a cut is always one space, so never mid-word. The merger press release stays as it is: it is a release, and its 8 lines over 400 characters are a business description, investor legends and the forward-looking-statements list, cautionary text rather than instrument terms (no key there is over 400 characters). Result (lines, over 400, over 800, over 1,500, longest): credit agreement 2,640 / 772 / 288 / 87 / 5,475 → 3,519 / 792 / 152 / 15 / 2,618; indenture 3,189 / 588 / 174 / 40 / 4,594 → 3,567 / 575 / 100 / 18 / 2,760; Fourth Amendment 108 / 20 / 5 / 1 / 2,378 → 113 / 21 / 3 / 1 / 1,600; Fifth Amendment 124 / 34 / 7 / 4 / 2,769 → 135 / 35 / 9 / 2 / 1,613; merger 8-K 124 / 26 / 5 / 1 / 3,430 → 158 / 25 / 2 / 0 / 1,279; 1,307 cuts in 605 sentences (604 comma-enumerator, 330 proviso, 154 comma-conjunction, 131 semicolon-enumerator, 54 semicolon-conjunction, 34 colon). The count over 400 barely moves (a cut can leave two pieces each over 400, and most of those lines are one unenumerated clause); the long tail falls. C4's 1,826-character line is cut into 467, 404, 290, 397 and 264 characters (the clause `(2)` lead-in, then `(a)`, its `(i)`/`(ii)` ratio test, `(b)`, its ratio test), its 957-character continuation into 306, 288 and 361; the page number still separates the two. Re-admitted, the 26 copies in the nine sets that carry any of the five (`czr-2026q2-covenant-refinancing`, `-full-credit-assessment`, `-liquidity`, `-lite-covenant-refinancing`, `-lite-full-credit-screen`, `-lite-portfolio`, `-lite-relative-value`, `-portfolio`, `-relative-value`; `grep -l` over the manifests gives the same nine) are replaced byte for byte; the CCL sets, `czr-2026q2` and `czr-2026q2-earnings-update` are untouched. Of the 23 key and alternative rows bound to the five, two keys moved, in 6 rows. The indenture's Section 4.08(a) line (800 characters; CP-4 of covenant-refinancing, full-credit-assessment, lite-covenant-refinancing, lite-full-credit-screen and portfolio) is restated as its first clause (518), which grants the `101%` repurchase right and ends `…this Section 4.08;`; the `provided, however,` proviso (281, no repurchase of Notes called for redemption) is the next line and is not keyed, as it carries none of the key's figures. The indenture's Change of Control clause (2) (876 characters; CP-3C of covenant-refinancing) is cut inside its enumerated exclusion parenthetical and restated as its second piece (695, from `(ii) any person or entity acting in its capacity as trustee` to the end), which carries the beneficial ownership of `more than 50%` of the voting Equity Interests; the 180-character opening (`(2) any “person” or “group” …`, exclusion `(i)`) is not keyed. No register or projection key names text. `RESULT.md` text hashes, notes and set digests, `COMMITTED_SET_DIGESTS`, the register notes (`documents.json`, "re-split by clause 4 October 2026 (F498)") and `DOCUMENTS.md` move with them. No byte count moved (a space became a line feed), so the large-file pins, the PR-size exclusions and the page-map threshold stand. Evidence: the converter run twice gives identical SHA-256s; `scratchpad/t6c/fidelity_v4.py` re-derives v3 from the raw HTML (equal to `scratchpad/edgar/text/` byte for byte), and for each of the five every v3 line equals its v4 pieces joined by one space, every cut is a boundary of the rule at the shallowest depth, a line of 400 or less or a table row is one unchanged line, no line is empty or space-edged, and the six other conversions are v3 byte for byte (bad = 0); `tests/test_qualification_on_disk.py` holds every key and alternative a whole unique line. CP-0's request (host code: `prepare_pack`, `gate_view`, `_evidence_section`, `encode_request`) grows by 0.4-0.7% in each set: the largest, `czr-2026q2-full-credit-assessment`, from 2,777,711 to 2,792,504 bytes, 66.6% of `MAX_REQUEST_BYTES` (4,194,304), its evidence section from 2,584,090 to 2,595,591 bytes; the largest source, the credit agreement at 935,517 bytes, is under `GATE_SOURCE_BYTES` (1,572,864), so no set has a page map.
- F499 (2026-10-04, live-loop fault L6, runs C4 CP-0 and C2 CP-4; D30's addendum class) — The host text check says which line and which character. In live run C4 (`caos_qualify_2d868ae1…`) CP-0 attempts 2 and 3 (`36b43ed8…`, `b645e014…`) and in C2 (`caos_qualify_b5549d83…`) CP-4 attempt 2 (`916c660b…`) were refused `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` and told only "the Markdown carries a control character other than a line feed or tab, or text that is not in Unicode NFC form". The stored bodies (`scratchpad/l6/probe.py`, read-only): C4 attempt 2 wrote U+001C (INFORMATION SEPARATOR FOUR) in place of every double quote of its front matter, lines 2-15 (14 lines, two each); attempt 3, carried attempt 2's answer back (D104, its `\u001c` escapes intact), swapped them for U+0002 (START OF TEXT) on the same 14 lines; attempt 4 wrote quotes and was accepted. C2 CP-4 attempt 2 wrote an ANSI colour sequence, U+001B (ESCAPE) then `[34;1m`, before `"CP-4"` on line 2, one line. Each previous attempt's answer had plain quotes, and no delivered evidence line, upstream handoff, authority file or feedback line of those prompts carries a control character (`scratchpad/l6/inputs.py`): the model introduced them, and D104's carried answer preserved C4's into attempt 3. No non-NFC text was found. Fix (`caos/methodology/handoff.py`, `_unclean_bound`): when `_clean` refuses, the line names the first offending line (1-based), the code point and its name (`unicodedata`'s, or the ISO 6429 name of a C0 control or DEL, or the code point alone), and how many lines carry one -- "line 2 of the Markdown carries U+001C (INFORMATION SEPARATOR FOUR), a control character other than a line feed or tab (14 lines in all); remove it"; text NFC would change gets "line N of the Markdown is not in Unicode NFC form (first at U+0065 U+0301; 1 line in all); write it in composed form". No answer text; across `BoundaryText` at `MAX_FEEDBACK_CHARS`, and the old unlocated words when it will not cross or nothing is located. Acceptance is unchanged: `_clean` still decides, and the same answers are refused with the same code. Evidence: `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_control_character_is_named_by_line_and_code_point_never_quoted`, `::test_text_not_in_nfc_is_named_by_line_and_the_code_points_nfc_changes`, `::test_an_unlocated_or_unbounded_text_check_keeps_the_unlocated_words`.
- D105 (2026-10-04, owner decision of 4 October 2026: "you do not need to display the entire line verbatim. just allow the user the ability to trace where a statement or conclusion was derived from.", then, asked to choose, "Yes, 8-word excerpts (Recommended)" for the citation rule and "Excerpt appears in the body (Recommended)" for the trace link; Task EX1 of `excerpt-plan.md`) — An answer's citation is an exact excerpt of one evidence line, not the whole line. **Rule** (`citations.EXCERPT`, `excerpt-of-shown-line`, `_excerpt_run`): `matched_text` anchors when it is a run of at least `MIN_EXCERPT_WORDS` (8) whole words of one evidence line as the evidence section showed it, or that whole line when it has fewer, found once on its cited page. The passes are `WHOLE_LINE`'s (as shown, NFC; edge punctuation forgiven, a figure keeping its parentheses and leading point, EV-4; the tracked-letter join on a tracking extractor), each counting every place on the page where the quote is an excerpt: two places, in one line or in two, are `CITATION_AMBIGUOUS`; a run onto the next line, or a quote that starts or ends inside a word, is `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED`; a quote under eight words can only be a whole line and is located exactly as `WHOLE_LINE` locates it. D94 applies as for `WHOLE_LINE` (`REANCHORING_RULES`): an excerpt of exactly one delivered line of exactly one other delivered page is anchored there, with `cited_page`. Delivery is judged on the one block the excerpt is in (R24-16). Invariant 11 holds: every excerpt anchors to one line coordinate or is refused. **AI-4** (N28's reason for `WHOLE_LINE`: a fragment that drops a qualifier such as "not", shown as a host-verified fact): the eight contiguous words within one line stop a qualifier dropped from inside the run, which then no longer matches, but not one at the excerpt's edge -- "believe that the Company will be able to refinance the Notes before maturity." is an excerpt of "We do not believe that …" (the fix-round-1 audit's probe). The guard for that case is the full source line shown around every excerpt, which the record now carries (`AnchoredCitation.line_text`, the shown line, its tokens NFC joined by one space) and which EX2 displays in the wire `CitationView`, the analysis read, the deliverable render and the evidence drawers; EX1 merges only together with EX2. **Records keep their rule:** a new record names `excerpt-of-shown-line` and writes `line_text` on every citation; an `ANY_RUN`, `WHOLE_LINE_AS_STORED` or `WHOLE_LINE` record writes none and is re-anchored by its own rule (`verify_stored_citations`, the proof, the freeze), so no stored byte moves; a line under another rule, or an excerpt record missing one, does not decode. **Prompt** (`final_check.md`): "`matched_text` copies an exact excerpt of one evidence line, character for character: at least 8 consecutive words, or the whole line if it has fewer. The excerpt stays within that one line and appears exactly once on its cited page; the same excerpt appears verbatim in the Markdown body after the front matter, beside the statement it supports, in the source's own wording"; D102's coverage sentence stays, citing "the excerpt", and its "the cited lines may be quoted together in one evidence section of the body" goes, as contrary to "beside the statement it supports". The host's verbatim-in-body check is unchanged and applies to the excerpt. **Retry hints** (D82): fewer than eight words of a longer line, "quotes fewer than 8 words of its line; quote at least 8 consecutive words, or the whole line if shorter" (the "part of a longer evidence line … quote the whole line" path is gone); ambiguous, "occurs more than once on its cited page; quote a longer excerpt that occurs once"; overrun (F496), now found from any word of the line and not only its first, "runs past the end of the evidence line of page N, which ends "…"; stop where the line ends"; another page, "is an excerpt of an evidence line of page N, not of its cited page"; the generic wording and F491's closing rule name the excerpt rule; near miss (F493), dropped cells (F495), unknown source (F495) and the keep list (F491) are unchanged and still fit (a whole line or row is an excerpt). **Search:** a page's shown lines end to end, their keys joined by NUL (which no stored token can hold) and found by `str.find`: 512 eight-word excerpts of a 240,000-token page of fourteen distinct words take about 0.3 s, 3.7 s by a Python step per partial match. **Replay** (`scratchpad/ex1/replay.py`, read-only, over the 25 run databases in the current store schema; the 44 older `public`-schema ones predate what this build reads, and their records are in the blob sweep): every one of the 44 accepted answers' records (all `WHOLE_LINE`) re-encodes to its own bytes and re-anchors unchanged by its own rule, and all 52 record blobs in the store round-trip (44 `WHOLE_LINE`, 8 `ANY_RUN`). Re-accepted under `EXCERPT`, 43 of the 44 anchor every citation at the same page, rectangles and cited page; one, E1 (`893f8839`) CP-0 attempt 2 citation 11, would refuse: a whole row cited on the wrong page, which D94 re-anchored, is also an excerpt of `Total Adjusted EBITDA | …` on another delivered page, so it has two places (four refused-answer citations meet the same case). Of the 150 citations refused `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` in stored answers, 58 anchor under `EXCERPT` -- among them C4 (`2d868ae1`) CP-4 citation 6, 159 and 179 words of the 1,826-character line in attempts 2 and 4 -- and 15 refused attempts would have anchored every citation; R4 (`556b9b9f`)'s three stay refused (a whole row cited on page 16 that is on pages 11 and 15, and two three-word `Total | $…` cells, now told they are too short). Evidence: `tests/test_excerpt_citations.py`, `tests/test_handoff_record.py::test_an_excerpt_record_keeps_the_line_of_each_citation`, `tests/test_canonical_proof.py::test_a_record_is_re_anchored_by_the_rule_it_was_accepted_under`, `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_an_excerpt_of_eight_words_is_accepted_and_keeps_its_line`, `::test_too_few_words_of_a_line_get_the_second_attempt_naming_it`, `::test_the_anchoring_line_places_each_unlocated_citation_it_can`, `tests/test_whole_line_citations.py::test_a_placed_citation_is_told_it_is_too_short_or_where_it_is`, `tests/test_handoff_invocation.py::test_the_prompt_repeats_the_closed_contract_after_evidence`. Supersedes N28/F235 for new answers. The display guard is delivered by F502: every surface that shows a host-verified citation shows its whole line, the excerpt marked.
- F500 (2026-10-04, Task EX1 and its fix round 1) — D105 is a deliberate departure from the legacy `prompt` parity group: the final check's citation sentence changed in all 27 cases (each 96 bytes longer than before D105; 33 shorter at EX1, then 129 longer at fix round 1, F501); no other group moves. Only the `prompt` group was regenerated, from the legacy snapshot's own code with the change mirrored (D34's procedure, `scratchpad/ex1/regen.sh`, first verified to reproduce the committed goldens byte for byte). Host pins regenerated by `scripts/host_manifest.py` (`final_check.md` 1,874 → 1,841 → 1,970 bytes).
- F501 (2026-10-04, Task EX1 fix round 1, the adversarial audit of D105; probes in `scratchpad/audit-ex1/`) — D105's search, hints, prompt and record, as the audit found them. **Search:** a hit `_one_excerpt` rejected (a run past its line, edges that did not stand for the quote's) was a Python step with no bound: 64 quotes of a 240,000-token page of one word in 7-word lines took 11.45 s (about 92 s per 512), 64 edge-rejected quotes of 120 lines of 2,000 words 15.7 s (about 126 s per 512). The joined keys now break between lines (NUL, SOH, NUL; `_LINE_BREAK`), so every hit is within one line; the edge pass searches one exact needle per pair of page keys standing for the quote's first and last word (`_Flat.edge_needles`), so every hit is a place and the second refuses `CITATION_AMBIGUOUS` at once; a quote with more than `MAX_EDGE_NEEDLES` (16) such pairs is refused `CITATION_AMBIGUOUS` rather than searched (fix round 2: refused `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED`, D94 read it as "no line here" and re-anchored it on another page with a false `cited_page`; `CITATION_AMBIGUOUS` is never re-anchored and is raised before the tracked-letter pass, so the refusal is the cap's), and one whose interior is in no line is found nowhere in one scan. The same pages now take 0.17 s and 0.16 s for 64 quotes; 512 ordinary excerpts of a 240,000-token page 0.34 s; the worst case under the bound (interior present, 16 needles that never hit) 2.8 s for 512. Anchoring is unchanged for every stored answer (the replay's counts are identical). **Hints:** an ambiguous quote that is a whole delivered line of its page is told "is a whole evidence line that appears more than once on its cited page, so it cannot be cited there; cite another line, or a longer excerpt where one exists" (`LineHint.repeated`, read from delivered text only); a quote whose unique run on its cited page crosses two delivered lines is told it "runs from one evidence line onto the next; quote within one line: at least 8 consecutive words of it, or the whole line if shorter" (`LineFinding.across`), not that it is short; an overrun keeping fewer than eight words of its line is told to "stop where the line ends, keeping at least 8 words of it, or quote the whole line" (`overrun_kept`). The replay places the straddle wording on 3 stored attempts and the short overrun on one; no stored answer is ambiguous under `EXCERPT`. F493's near miss for a mid-line excerpt is N138. **Prompt:** a whole short line is copied "including any leading bullet or footnote marker and any trailing `|`" again, and "beside the statement it supports (in its Evidence Trace row or the sentence that states it)" meets the canon's `## Evidence Trace`. **Record:** `record_bytes` holds a record to `_lines_held` too, so a line under another rule or missing under `EXCERPT` raises `ValueError` instead of being dropped; and a stored line must hold its quote (`within_line`), checked when read and written -- all 52 stored records pass; that it is the line the quote anchors in stays the proof's re-anchoring. Fix round 2: `within_line` had trimmed only the ends of the whole quote, while edge forgiveness strips each side of the first and last word, so `2025 revenue rose 4% to $1.2 billion on higher` (from "In 2025, revenue rose …"), `Company shall maintain …` (from `(the "Company") shall maintain …`) and a whole line `...` anchored and then could not be written, a `ValueError` inside `_answer` that stopped the billed run as a host fault (and `replay_billed` likewise). It is now anchoring's own test, `_excerpt_run` over the line alone, each word a token (edge forgiveness with a figure's marks, NFC, the tracked-letter join; found twice in the line is still within it), and a record `record_bytes` refuses is refused `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` in the answer path (`canonical._written`), never a `ValueError`; a property test holds `within_line` to every quote `EXCERPT` anchors on a generated page. **Tests** kill the audit's three surviving mutants: the cross-line run (now the missing line break), `_not_a_line_of` judging `WHOLE_LINE` for an `EXCERPT` record, and the edge pass before the exact one; and, at fix round 2, a straddle named over a never-delivered next line, the short-overrun flag never set, the cap returning None, and the edge-key lookup without its NFC branch (14 mutants run, all killed). Evidence: `tests/test_excerpt_citations.py::test_an_excerpt_never_borrows_a_word_from_the_line_before`, `::test_an_exact_excerpt_is_found_before_an_edge_forgiven_one`, `::test_near_places_cost_one_scan_each_on_adversarial_pages`, `::test_edge_forgiveness_stops_at_its_needle_bound`, `::test_the_retry_helpers_read_a_line_as_anchoring_does`, `::test_an_excerpt_cited_on_the_wrong_page_is_anchored_at_its_true_page`, `tests/test_whole_line_citations.py::test_a_repeated_line_or_a_straddle_is_told_what_it_is`, `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_the_anchoring_line_tells_a_repeated_line_a_straddle_and_a_short_overrun`, `tests/test_handoff_record.py::test_an_excerpt_record_keeps_the_line_of_each_citation`, `tests/test_canonical_proof.py::test_a_record_is_re_anchored_by_the_rule_it_was_accepted_under`; fix round 2: `tests/test_excerpt_citations.py::test_a_stored_line_holds_every_quote_anchoring_accepts`, `::test_within_line_accepts_every_excerpt_anchoring_accepts`, `::test_a_capped_quote_is_never_re_anchored_on_another_page`, `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_record_whose_lines_do_not_hold_is_a_typed_refusal`.
- F502 (2026-10-04, Task EX2 of `excerpt-plan.md`; EX1's adversarial review, Critical; D105) — The full source line is shown wherever an excerpt is shown as verified, and answer keys are scored by the line a citation anchored in. **Display (AI-4's guard).** D105 rests AI-4 on the trace view showing the whole line around an excerpt, and every display showed the excerpt alone, so eight words could drop a "not", a "provided that", an "Except as" or a row's label just outside them and read as a host-verified fact. The deliverable (`caos/deliverable/render.py` `_citation`, the source facts and, through `_figure_line`, each narrative figure, which reads the line of the record citation it is bound to) shows the record's `line_text` in the blockquote with the excerpt in `<mark>` (also bold, styled on the element, so it survives a print that drops backgrounds and a page with no excerpt keeps its stylesheet: the legacy `render` parity goldens do not move), every character escaped; a record from before D105 keeps no line and renders byte for byte as before (nothing is marked without a line, even a whole-line quote padded with spaces); the verifier pins the new renderer (`RENDERER_SHA256`). `render.traced_line` places the excerpt as the host anchored it, within the one line (`citations._excerpt_run`'s three passes: NFC words equal; edge punctuation forgiven, a figure keeping its parentheses and leading point; tracked letters joined), its edge constants restated (the renderer is the standard library alone) and held equal to `citations`' by a test; a pass that finds two places, or none, marks nothing and the line is shown whole, unmarked. On the wire (`caos/api/wire.py`), `CitationView` and `NarrativeFigure` gain a required `line: LineView` (`before`, `excerpt`, `after`; their concatenation is the line, split by the server so no client slices by offsets across UTF-16), and `recorded`, built by `LineView.of(line_text, matched_text, rule)`: the Analysis read (`reads/analysis.py` `_citation`), the Book (whose passport citations are Analysis's source facts) and the Report read (`reads/reports.py` `_figure`, from the bound record citation); a whole-line record's quote is its line (`before` and `after` empty, recorded); an `ANY_RUN` record's quote is any unique run of its page and no line (219 of the 263 stored records, 2,574 citations), so `recorded` is false and every workspace display labels it "Quote (source line not recorded)", unmarked and never called a source line (EX2 review, fix round 1). `frontend/src/wire/v1/schema.json` is regenerated, and `documents.ts` validates `LineView` closed. In the workspace one component, `frontend/src/evidence/TracedLine.tsx`, renders the line with the excerpt in a `<mark>`: Analysis's source facts (`AnalysisSection.tsx`), the source drawer (`SourceDrawer.tsx`), the evidence drawer (`EvidenceDrawer.tsx`; a passport citation passes its line, one with none is its own line, `wholeLine`) and a saved narrative's figures in Report and Committee (`Narrative.tsx`); the drawers' label is "Source line · the cited excerpt marked"; the mark takes its surface's ink (`color: inherit`), which Committee's paper needs in the dark scheme (axe: 720 entries, 0 violations). The report picker's options and the draft's figure list (`FilingControls.tsx`, `figures.choiceText`) name a citation by its source line, the quote in guillemets (`line_text`, or the quote under a whole-line rule), or as "quote (source line not recorded)", and a narrative chip's accessible name reads the line, or the quote so labelled. The eight CZR `RESULT.md` notes that said a fragment could never be met now say a key is met by any citation anchored in its line (`RESULT.md` is in no set digest). **Long lines: shown whole**, not elided: `line_text` is the shown block, at most `GROUP_WIDTH` (4,096) characters, so the whole of it always fits a wrapped blockquote. The audit's caveat stands: a token line wider than 4,096 characters is shown as several blocks, and an excerpt in a later block is shown without the qualifier that may sit in the block before it. Measured after 6c (`scratchpad/ex2/longlines.py`, every committed set's 29 distinct documents, 38,235 token lines): 3 lines exceed 4,096 characters -- the BA FY2025 10-K (one line of 71,278), the F FY2025 10-K (105,991) and the NCLH Q4 2025 release (7,095); none in any CZR document (N139). **Scoring by line.** `proof.cited_line` is the line a citation anchored in: an `EXCERPT` record's `line_text`, or the quote of a record accepted under an earlier rule (scored as it always was). The proof's `anchored` set is `(module, document, line)`, and `matrix._matches` and `scripts/quality_compare.py` read it, so a key (one whole line, F475) or an alternative (D101) is met by any excerpt of its line under its module. Re-extracted over the baseline and the R3, R5, L1 and E1 records (`scratchpad/ex2/keyreplay.py`, record digests read from the run databases read-only), the head's scoring and this one agree on all 82 key verdicts. **K2, the E1 finding.** `tests/test_qualification_on_disk.py`'s committed-set check no longer asks that a key or alternative be a unique run of its document, only exactly one whole evidence line (and NFC, as a shown line is): the unique-run test predates F475 and is redundant under scoring by line. The one alternative K1 rejected for that reason alone, the 10-Q's `Adjusted EBITDA | $920 | $955 | $1,807 | $1,839` (the consolidated line its segment note reconciles net loss to; its words recur inside the MD&A's `Total Adjusted EBITDA` row), is added beside the release's row in `czr-2026q2` (CP-L10), `-earnings-update` and `-full-credit-assessment` (CP-1B), `-lite-full-credit-screen`, `-lite-portfolio`, `-lite-relative-value` and `-relative-value` (CP-1C); the one-figure `Cash and cash equivalents | $965` rows stay out (no comparative). The seven `RESULT.md` files, their digests and `COMMITTED_SET_DIGESTS` move. E1's stored proof replayed through the matrix, read-only (`scratchpad/ex2/e1replay.py`): 2/5 → 3/5, its CP-L10 Adjusted EBITDA key now met; no other verdict moves. Evidence: `tests/test_qualification_on_disk.py::test_an_excerpt_meets_the_key_its_line_is_under_the_keys_module`, `::test_a_record_from_before_excerpts_is_scored_by_its_quote`, `::test_every_committed_answer_key_names_its_route_and_exact_source`, `::test_a_partial_alternative_is_not_one_whole_evidence_line`; `tests/test_deliverable_render.py::test_an_excerpt_is_shown_marked_inside_its_whole_line`, `::test_a_narrative_figure_shows_the_line_of_the_citation_it_names`, `::test_a_line_that_is_not_text_is_refused`, `::test_traced_line_places_the_excerpt_as_the_host_anchored_it`, `::test_the_edge_punctuation_is_the_anchors_own`, `::test_a_record_without_a_line_renders_byte_for_byte_as_before`; `tests/test_wire_contract.py::test_a_citation_carries_the_whole_line_its_excerpt_is_marked_in`; `tests/test_analysis_section.py::test_an_excerpt_is_served_inside_the_whole_line_it_anchored_in`; `tests/test_revision_sections.py` (the report figure's line); `frontend/tests/unit/evidence.test.tsx` "the drawer shows the whole source line, the cited excerpt marked (D105)", `evidence-page.test.tsx` "the drawer shows the whole source line with the cited excerpt marked" and the committee figure, `analysis.test.tsx` "source facts name the file, page, matched text and withdrawn state" and "a fact whose record holds no line is labelled a quote, never marked", `report-figures.test.tsx` `test_the_picker_names_each_citation_by_its_line_or_as_a_bare_quote`, `wire-contract.test.ts` (a line closed and required). **Display superseded (F506, D107):** the owner's "Compact, source on click" moves the full line, its excerpt marked, into the source drawers alone; the deliverable, Analysis's source facts, the evidence lists, a narrative figure and the picker show the excerpt compact, and the page carries no `<mark>`. `LineView` stays on the wire for the drawer; scoring by line stands.
- D106 (2026-10-04, owner decision of 4 October 2026, given in session after another session relayed its recommendation; Task NB1 of `nonblocking-plan.md`; builds on D105) — A citation fault refuses the citation, never the answer. **Rulings, quoted:** to "…a citation fault no longer refuses or retries a module … Is that your decision? If so, what happens to a citation that does not anchor?", "Yes; show as unverified (Recommended)" -- kept, labelled "unverified – page N" with the model's own locator, its claim Deploy V's lineage class "Untraced" (`CANON_SHARED.md`, CP_CANONICAL_EVIDENCE_CLASSIFICATION; VE-015); committee prose, "Labelled unverified too (Recommended)"; invariant 11, "Keep text; rule in the Dn (Recommended)"; scoring, "Anchored only (Recommended)". Evidence relayed and accepted: citation refusals took 44% of model time on 2 October and 26-71% of wall time in the completed 3 October runs; every analysed one was a copy or format slip and none caught a wrong figure; the owner validates output against the sources personally; the canon asks only for "source title, period, page/section/line if available". **Invariant 11, read:** "coordinate-anchored or refused" holds at citation level -- "refused" applies to the citation, never to the answer; the text of invariant 11 is unchanged. **Verdict** (`canonical._answer`, `_partitioned`, `_judged`): `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED`, `CITATION_AMBIGUOUS`, `CITATION_NOT_DELIVERED` (`handoff.UNVERIFIED_CODES`) and the verbatim-in-body check no longer refuse an answer every structural check passes; `parse_response` refuses only a transport that is not the closed one and returns whether the body carries each quote. Each citation is judged alone under `EXCERPT`, in order: anchored, host-verified as D105 (one the body does not carry stays anchored, `AnchoredCitation.linked` false, "not linked to a statement"), or unverified (`UnverifiedCitation`: the model's `source_id`, page and quote as it crosses `BoundaryText`, and the code that left it so). An answer with zero anchored citations is accepted; the final check's "at least one citation in all" stays in the prompt. **Prompt** (F503): `instruction.md` no longer lists the citation rule under "Rules that will cause your answer to be refused if broken"; after that list it says "Every citation follows the one citation rule stated in the final response check after the evidence; no other rule is stated. A citation the host cannot locate does not refuse your answer: it is kept as unverified and shown to the reader as such.", and `final_check.md` adds, after the rule, "A citation the host cannot locate is kept as unverified and shown to the reader as such; it does not refuse the answer." The rule itself (an exact excerpt of at least 8 words, verbatim in the body) is unchanged. Still refused exactly as before: a transport or citation structure that is not the wire's (keys, types, over `MAX_CITATIONS`, a repeat), the host text checks (F499) -- and, new, an unverified quote that will not cross `BoundaryText` or hides text (`HANDOFF_MALFORMED`, told by number, `_uncrossed_line`) -- front matter and identity, size (F494), demand (F497), forecast inputs, a record `record_bytes` refuses, and a store or evidence fault met while anchoring (raised, never a citation's). **Blocked** (coordinator's ruling on the owner's decision, NB1 follow-up): a validated Blocked handoff stands as Blocked whatever its quotes anchored, on its first attempt, with no retry. c-5b's guard (P3-2) honoured Blocked only once every quote anchored, so a Blocked verdict resting on a quote no evidence carried could not end a run; it existed because a citation fault was then a refusal, retried, and a Blocked answer was held to the same rule before it ended the run. Under D106 no citation fault refuses an answer, and the owner validates output against the sources himself, so the guard no longer applies: the Blocked answer's quotes are judged as any answer's (one that cannot be kept still refuses `HANDOFF_MALFORMED`), and every structural check a Blocked answer met before still holds. A Blocked answer writes no record and its body stays the attempt's diagnostic; since the audit round (owner: "Show its quotes (Recommended)", "The blocked view lists the answer's quotes, each marked verified or unverified … No new refusal") its citations as judged are kept too: **shape for NB2** -- `run_blocking_verdicts.citations_sha256` (migration `0044_blocking_citations`, nullable, CHECK hex-64; the row stays insert-only under 0021's triggers, written in the same insert that ends the run, `store.runs.BlockingVerdict`) addresses a blob `{"format": "caos-blocked-citations-v1", "citation_rule": "excerpt-of-shown-line", "citations": [...], "unverified": [...]}`, canonical JSON, both lists always present, each entry exactly as a record writes it (anchored: `document_sha256`, `page`, `matched_text`, `bboxes`, `line_text`, `cited_page` only when set, `linked` only when false; unverified: `source_id`, `page`, `matched_text`, `code`, `linked` only when false), read by `handoff.read_blocked_citations(blobs, sha)` (`ARTIFACT_RECORD_MISMATCH` for any other form); NULL for a verdict recorded before 0044. `_answer` returns a `BlockedAnswer` carrying those bytes; the runtime re-derives it through `replay_billed` (`Replayed.citations`) and stores the blob before the transition (`_block_on`; a blob that will not write is `STORE_UNAVAILABLE` and nothing moves). **Calculation inputs** (owner, audit round: "Calc inputs must anchor (Recommended)", "A citation that feeds the forecast calculator is treated as structural. If it fails to anchor, the module that wrote it gets a guided retry naming it. Every other citation stays non-blocking."): on a route that runs CP-CF, an unverified citation of CP-1, CP-2G or CP-4 that CP-CF will bind (`forecast.binds_input`: a line stating a value of a section that module owns, `validate_forecast_bindings`' own `_owner`/`_assignment` form) that CP-CF could not bind -- unverified, or anchored but not in the owner's Markdown as written (`forecast.carries`, the binder's raw-substring test, re-review fix round 2) -- refuses the owner's answer `HANDOFF_INCOMPLETE`, after size and demand and the same producer-guard shape as F497, and its guided retry names it by number (`_calculation_line`); the audit's wedge (CP-4 accepted on slipped binding quotes, CP-CF refused, the run held at 9 of 10) now retries and completes. No stored run pins CP-CF (25 of 25), so the replay moves no verdict. **Retries** (amends D82 and its amendment, and D30): anchoring's codes left `SECOND_ATTEMPT_CODES`; the four structural codes keep their three guided retries. A retry another check earned still carries the anchoring line and the body-quote line, each closed by `handoff.ADVISORY` ("advisory only: the host keeps such a citation, marked unverified or not linked to a statement, and does not refuse an answer for it"); neither earns one. An unverified quote longer than any evidence line (`GROUP_WIDTH`, 4,096 characters) cannot be kept and refuses `HANDOFF_MALFORMED`, named by number (M5). `scripts/rollback_check.py` refuses a rollback across a change of `handoff.RECORD_CODEC_VERSION` (1 since D106; an older build has none and refuses these records), M4. **Record** (`CanonicalRecord.unverified`, `record_bytes`, `_decoded_record`, `_held`): its own list, never mixed into `citations`, so `_lines_held` holds the anchored EXCERPT citations alone (the EX1 review's precondition); written only when it has entries and `linked` only when false, so every stored record is the same bytes; at least one citation of either kind and at most `MAX_CITATIONS` of both; either field outside `EXCERPT`, an empty list, a `linked` other than false, a code outside `UNVERIFIED_CODES` or a quote not as it crosses the boundary does not read. **Readers:** `verification._reanchored` (the proof's and the freeze's) locates the anchored list only and carries `linked` as recorded; `OrchestrationProof.unverified` carries each record's unverified citations by module, untouched, and `citations` counts only what was re-located (it may now be zero); answer keys are scored from `proof.anchored` alone; answer keys are met only by an anchored citation the body carries (owner, audit round: "Linked only (Recommended)"; `proof.scored_lines`, used by the proof's `anchored` set and by `scripts/quality_compare.py`), and an unverified citation records the body check too (`UnverifiedCitation.linked`, written only when false, NB2 review); the performed snapshot and quality_compare carry the unverified count only (M8); the upstream citation register lists anchored citations only, so downstream nodes see only verified citations in the host register, while the upstream handoff's body still carries its excerpts; a record holding none renders its section empty (`build_handoff_prompt` no longer refuses it). **Qualification:** no committed set declares a citation refusal (only `HANDOFF_BLOCKED` is declarable, DQ-2), so no case is restated or retired and no digest moves; the harness test that relied on a run-ending `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` now stops on `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`. **Replay** (`scratchpad/nb1/replay.py`, read-only, the 25 current-schema run databases; the 7 runs on today's build (C1-C4, E1, L1, R5) re-judged by `_answer` itself, the rest derived from the stage their stored code proves the old verdict reached): all 44 stored accepted answers' records re-encode to their own bytes and re-anchor through the new path; re-judged, 42 accept with the same anchored citations and none unverified, E1 CP-0 attempt 2 accepts with the one citation EX1 found no excerpt (now unverified), and C3 CP-0 attempt 1 is refused by F497's demand check, which postdates it. Of the refused attempts, 44 now accept: 41 `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` (CP-0 16, CP-1 8, CP-L10 5, CP-5 5, CP-1B 3, CP-2 2, CP-4 2; among them R5 CP-2 #1, CP-1B #2, CP-5 #1-2, C1 CP-1 #3, C2 CP-4 #1, C4 CP-0 #1 and CP-4 #4, E1 CP-0 #1, CP-L10 #1, CP-5 #1-2), 1 `CITATION_NOT_DELIVERED` (C1 CP-1 #1, 38 citations unverified) and 2 `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` from the body check (C4 CP-1 #1, C3 CP-2D #1, 5 citations each not linked); 2 more `CITATION_NOT_LOCATED` meet F497, C1 CP-1 #2 (`CITATION_NOT_DELIVERED`) meets the completeness checker it never reached, and R4's attempts stay refused (structural, or undetermined where its body check fired before validation on an older build). **Display** (Task NB2, F504; owner: "Yes; show as unverified", committee prose "Labelled unverified too"): an unverified citation is shown on every surface as the model's own -- labelled before its text "unverified – page N · the model's quote · claim lineage: Untraced · <not located | ambiguous | not in the delivered evidence>", its quote escaped, never in the host's well, never marked, never called a source line and never opened in a drawer (it has no line, rectangle or located document); the deliverable lists anchored citations alone under "Source facts (host-verified citations)" and the rest under "Unverified citations (the model's own locators and quotes)"; a module whose every citation is unverified renders, and only one citing nothing is refused `DELIVERABLE_UNCITED_FIGURE`; an anchored citation with `linked` false is shown as verified with "not linked to a statement in the answer". A committee figure may name an unverified citation (`{"unverified": {"route_node_id", "unverified_index"}}`) and is rendered labelled; a digit in prose that references nothing still refuses `NARRATIVE_FIGURE_UNREFERENCED`. A validated Blocked answer's quotes are shown with its verdict, compact, each marked verified or unverified, so the owner can check the block against the sources ("Show its quotes (Recommended)"; F504's fix round). Evidence: `tests/test_nonblocking_citations.py`, `tests/test_forecast_route.py::test_a_calculation_input_that_does_not_anchor_gets_a_guided_retry`, `::test_binds_input_reads_the_owner_and_the_assignment_form`, `tests/test_rollback_check.py::test_a_release_whose_record_codec_moved_cannot_be_rolled_back`, `tests/test_canonical_execution.py::test_one_unanchorable_quote_is_kept_unverified_beside_the_anchored_one`, `::test_a_quote_outside_the_captured_blocks_is_kept_unverified`, `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_citation_fault_alone_earns_no_retry`, `::test_too_few_words_of_a_line_get_the_second_attempt_naming_it`, `::test_the_ledger_makes_the_fourth_attempt_the_last_guided_retry`, `tests/test_lite_route_e2e_negative.py::test_an_undelivered_citation_never_reaches_the_proof_as_anchored`, `tests/test_loop_charges.py::test_a_module_whose_citations_cannot_be_anchored_still_completes`, `tests/test_canonical_runtime.py::test_a_blocked_handoff_with_an_unverified_quote_stands_as_blocked`, `tests/test_canonical_execution.py::test_billing_survives_every_analytical_refusal`, `tests/test_upstream_citation_register.py::test_a_register_must_cover_exactly_the_direct_upstream`.
- F503 (2026-10-04, Task NB1 follow-up; D106) — D106's prompt sentences are a deliberate departure from the legacy `prompt` parity group: all 27 cases grow by 186 bytes (`instruction.md` 1,422 → 1,488 bytes, `final_check.md` 1,970 → 2,090); no other group moves. Only the `prompt` group was regenerated, from the legacy snapshot's own code with the change mirrored (D34's procedure, `scratchpad/nb1/regen.sh`, first verified to reproduce the committed goldens byte for byte).
