- F504 (2026-10-04, Task NB2 of `nonblocking-plan.md`; D106; EX2's review minors) — D106's display, and the two minors EX2's re-review left. **Deliverable** (`caos/deliverable/render.py`): `_Handoff` reads the record's `unverified` list (absent is none) and its `citation_rule` (absent is `ANY_RUN`); the source facts list the anchored citations alone ("None: no citation of this module was located by the host." where there are none), then, only where there are any, "Unverified citations (the model's own locators and quotes)", each `unverified()`: `<p class="cite">unverified – page N · the model's quote · claim lineage: Untraced · <reason> · source <the model's source_id></p>` before the quote in `<blockquote style="border-left-style:dashed">`, every character escaped, no `<mark>` (`UNVERIFIED_REASONS`; an entry with another code, a page under 1 or no text is `DELIVERABLE_PAYLOAD_INVALID`); a module refuses `DELIVERABLE_UNCITED_FIGURE` only when both lists are empty; a citation with `linked` false keeps its line and mark and its cite line ends " · not linked to a statement in the answer". **Committee rule** (`caos/deliverable/revisions.py` `_REFERENCES`, `_resolved`, `_reference`): a span is prose, `{"figure": {"route_node_id", "citation_index"}}` or `{"unverified": {"route_node_id", "unverified_index"}}`, the host copying `source_id`, `page`, `matched_text` and `code` from the record's `unverified` entry; the page renders it as above; `prove_revision` re-derives both kinds; `verify_package` holds an unverified figure to the record entry it names (`_FIGURES`); a bare digit still refuses at save and in the portable check. `_span` fell under the complexity cap (16 -> 12; baselined functions 16 -> 15). **Wire** (`caos/api/wire.py`): `HandoffView.unverified_facts` (`UnverifiedCitationView`: `source_id`, `page`, `matched_text`, `code` and nothing else -- no store lookup, since a model's source_id may name no source the run was given), `CitationView.linked`, `NarrativeSpan.unverified` (`NarrativeUnverified`, with the record digest its node carries) and `NarrativeDraft.unverified` (`NarrativeUnverifiedRef`); `commands/deliverable._span` holds a draft span to exactly one kind; schema regenerated. The Book passport cites anchored citations only -- a CP-CF binding's quote must be one of its owner's anchored citations (`forecast.validate_forecast_bindings`) -- and carries `linked`. **Workspace**: `frontend/src/evidence/Unverified.tsx` (`unverifiedLabel`, `UNVERIFIED_REASONS`, `NOT_LINKED`, `UnverifiedFacts`): Analysis's Audit tab has an "Unverified citations" part after the source facts (a source named by its file where a located citation of the run names it, else by id), the module's caveats count them, the source facts of an all-unverified module say none was located; `SourceFacts`, `SourceDrawer` and the passport's `EvidenceDrawer` say "Not linked to a statement in the answer."; `Narrative.tsx` labels an unverified figure with no chip; the report picker offers each record's unverified citations, labelled, as `[CP-0 unverified #1]`. **EX2's minors.** The deliverable labels an `ANY_RUN` quote "Quote (source line not recorded)" (`<p class="cite">` above its blockquote), as the workspace does; whole-line and `EXCERPT` records are unlabelled. This is a deliberate departure from the legacy `render` parity group: the 13 cases that render (all but the refusals and `canonical_bound`) gain that label before each citation's blockquote (`narrative_spans` twice), and nothing else moves; only that group was regenerated, from the legacy snapshot with the label mirrored into its `_citation` (D34's procedure, `scratchpad/nb2/mirror_render.py`, the unmirrored snapshot first reproducing the committed goldens byte for byte); `RENDERER_SHA256` re-pinned. The picker (`figures.choiceText`) places its guillemets by `wordPlace`, the quote's NFC words as consecutive whole words of the line at exactly one place, never by `indexOf`; none, or two, and the line is shown unmarked. Evidence: `tests/test_unverified_citations_shown.py` (render, escaping through the EX2 injection probes, the all-unverified module, `linked`, the `ANY_RUN` label, the portable check, the save boundary over a real route), `tests/test_unverified_citations_served.py` (the Analysis read, the view's fields, the Report read, `_spans`); `frontend/tests/unit/unverified.test.tsx`, `report-figures.test.tsx` `test_the_picker_offers_unverified_citations_labelled_as_the_models_own`, `test_the_picker_marks_a_quote_by_whole_words_never_by_substring`; the demo fixture carries one unverified citation (CP-1B) and one unlinked fact (CP-1), and axe reports 0 violations over 720 entries. **Fix round 1** (the NB2 adversarial review, probes in `scratchpad/audit-nb2/`; NB1's fix round merged first, 905ed22). *Important 1, a stored long quote:* the reviewer's 70,011-character quote failed the Analysis and Report reads with a `ValidationError` (`INTERNAL_FAULT`). As an unverified quote it can no longer be written (NB1's `GROUP_WIDTH` cap) and a record holding one does not decode, so every read and `save_revision` answer `ARTIFACT_RECORD_MISMATCH`; as an anchored quote (the codec bounds no quote's length; an `ANY_RUN` quote is any run of its page) every read serves it, and each part of its `LineView`, cut at `QUOTE_CHARS` ending "…(truncated)" (`wire.bounded`, `TRUNCATED`): the Analysis, Model and Book reads over HTTP and the Report and Committee figure builders (`reports._figure`, `_unverified`) with the probe (`tests/test_quote_bound_reads.py`). *Important 2, a11y:* report-v1, committee-v1 and report.acts carry an unverified record entry, an unverified narrative span and an unlinked figure, and a demo state `run?fixture=blocked` a Blocked answer's quotes; the gate opens the Audit tab of CP-1 and CP-1B (`open=audit`) and picks the report picker's unverified option (`pick=unverified`) before it scans: 816 entries (720 before), 0 violations; labels on Committee's paper take `--paper-meta` (the theme's muted ink was 2.36:1 on cream in the dark scheme). *Minors:* a committee figure shows its record entry's `linked` -- the deliverable reads it from the record (`render._named`, `_figure`), the wire's `NarrativeFigure`, `NarrativeUnverified` and `UnverifiedCitationView` carry it, and the drawer no longer forces it -- and an unverified quote the answer does not carry says "not linked to a statement in the answer" beside its reason (generic, D107: no marker names it); the chrome counts "N verified, M unverified citations" and a module with an unverified citation takes RESTRICTED's ring, never SUCCESS (`tone.handoffSeverity`). *The Blocked view* (owner, audit round: "Show its quotes (Recommended)"; compact per D107): `BlockedByView` gains `quotes_recorded`, `quotes_refusal`, `verified` (`BlockedQuoteView`: document, its pinned source and withdrawal, page, quote, line, linked) and `unverified`, served by Run and Analysis through one builder (`analysis.blocked_by_view`) from `read_blocked_citations` and `cited_source_ids` (one blob and one query more, only on a BLOCKED run; both budgets +1); a NULL `citations_sha256` reads "Quotes not recorded for this block.", and a blob missing or not this host's bytes is its typed refusal, contained in the view, never the page's. The workspace lists them as document · page · a short excerpt (twelve words), each located one a chip opening the source drawer (resolved by the verdict's attempt) where its line is shown with the excerpt marked, each unverified one labelled. Evidence: `tests/test_quote_bound_reads.py`, `tests/test_blocked_quotes_served.py`, `tests/test_unverified_citations_shown.py::test_a_committee_figure_shows_the_records_linked`; `frontend/tests/unit/unverified.test.tsx` (the Blocked view, its drawer resolution, the unlinked committee figure, the run panel), `compose.test.tsx` (counts, ring). **Display superseded (F506, D107):** the deliverable's unverified section, its dashed blockquote and the source-facts list become one compact Citations appendix per module, each unverified citation a line labelled first; the workspace's unverified list and Blocked quotes are clamped to about one line, with their markers. The labels, `linked`, the committee rule and the escaping stand.
- D107 (2026-10-04, owner decision of 4 October 2026; Task MK1 of `markers-plan.md`; builds on D105 and D106) — An answer's body cites by numbered markers, `[C<n>]`, and quotes no source text; the excerpt lives only in its citations list. **Owner, verbatim:** "Complete the 5 items. Note the app should not have large bodies of citation text buried with analysis. The user can open the source documents to view these. The should focused on the analysis not embedding large bodies of source text." Asked, the owner chose "Numbered markers (Recommended)", whose option read "The body writes [C1], [C2]… beside each statement, and the excerpts stay in the citations list. CAOS checks every marker matches a citation, and clicking one opens the source document at that line. Evidence Trace becomes a short locator table: document and page, no quotes.", and "Compact, source on click (Recommended)", whose option read "Show document · page · the short excerpt. Clicking opens the source with the full line highlighted, so a dropped qualifier is still one click away. The deliverable lists citations compactly in an appendix rather than inline full lines." **Supersedes** D105's trace link ("Excerpt appears in the body": the excerpt verbatim in the body beside its statement) and F502's inline full line, which MK2 replaces (AI-4's guard becomes the source view: the full line, highlighted, one click away); **amends** D106's `linked`, which now means a marker names the citation. **Rule** (`handoff.markers`, `_MARKER`): a marker is exactly `[C` + 1-9 ASCII digits + `]`, or several in one bracket, `[C2, C5]` (each with its `C`, separated by a comma and at most one space), and names the citation at that 1-based place of the list; it is read after the front matter, with the body's backslash escapes read as the marks they write (F149, so `\[C3\]` is `[C3]`), and inside fenced code too (a fence is text the reader sees). Anything else is text, never a marker and never refused: `[c3]`, `[C 3]`, `[C3-C5]`, `[C3–C5]`, `[C3,4]`, `[C]`, a Unicode digit; the retry's advisory marker line counts such near misses (`_NEAR_MARKER`: a bracket holding `c` or `C` and a digit, spaces allowed between, up to 24 more characters) and says to write `[C3]`, or `[C3, C4]` for several. **Checks** (`parse_response`, `feedback_lines`): a marker that names no citation (`n < 1` or `n > len(citations)`) refuses the answer `HANDOFF_MALFORMED`, structural, as which citation it meant is undecidable (a Blocked answer included, like every structural check); its guided retry is told each such marker once, as `[C<n>]`, and the count: "host marker check: the Markdown body writes [C9], but the answer has 8 citations, so it names none; a marker [C<n>] names the citation at place n of the list, from [C1] to [C8]" (`_dangling_line`). A citation no marker names is kept, `linked: false`, and never refuses (D106); the advisory line names it by number (`_unmarked_line`, closed by `ADVISORY`), where the body-quote line used to. The verbatim-in-body check and its machinery (`_quoted`, `_carried`, `_openings`, `_wears`, `_body_words`; F148, F149, F476, R24-09) are removed with their tests: nothing reads them any more. **Record** (`AnchoredCitation.marker`, `UnverifiedCitation.marker`, `_markers_held`; `RECORD_CODEC_VERSION` 1 to 2, so `scripts/rollback_check.py` refuses a rollback across it): every citation accepted since D107, anchored or unverified, keeps its place in the answer's list, together the places 1 to n, each list ascending; it is written on every such citation and on none before, in the Blocked answer's citations blob as in a record (`blocked_citations_bytes`), and the proof and freeze carry it as recorded (`verification._reanchored`). Without it an anchored citation's marker could not be known once an unverified one was split out of the list (D106). **Stored records are never re-judged:** a record accepted before D107 keeps its `linked` with its old meaning, the excerpt verbatim in its body, and holds no `marker`; its bytes are unchanged and it reads as before. **Upstream register** (`invocation._citation_register`): each line of a record accepted since D107 opens `marker: [C<n>]`, the marker the upstream body cites it by, so a downstream model can resolve a marker it reads there; the header says so ("its marker is the [C<n>] by which that handoff's body cites it"); an unverified citation is still not listed. **CP-CF** (the NB1 review's coupling note): `forecast.carries`, the raw body-substring test, is removed. `validate_forecast_bindings` binds a value to an owner's anchored citation that a marker names (`linked`, from the owner's verified record), and searches neither handoff for the quote; the calc-input guard (`canonical._unbindable`) holds the same test, so an owner's binding citation that is unverified or unmarked refuses the owner `HANDOFF_INCOMPLETE`, and `_calculation_line` tells it "must be an exact excerpt of one evidence line that anchors, named by its [C<n>] marker in the Markdown body" beside the anchoring and marker lines. **Scoring** (`proof.scored_lines`): keys are met by anchored citations a marker names; an older record is scored by its own `linked`. **Prompt** (F505): `final_check.md`'s "the same excerpt appears verbatim in the Markdown body after the front matter, beside the statement it supports (in its Evidence Trace row or the sentence that states it), in the source's own wording ("we", "our", "us"), never rephrased into the third person. Cite the excerpt behind each material figure and each statement that a register row or conclusion rests on, at least one citation in all, and only excerpts that support a claim you wrote." is now "The excerpt lives only in `citations`: the Markdown body quotes no source text and cites by marker, `[C1]` for the first citation in the list, `[C2]` for the second, `[C2, C5]` for several, beside the statement each supports. Put a marker beside each material figure and each statement that a register row or conclusion rests on, at least one citation in all, and cite only excerpts that support a claim you wrote. Evidence Trace is a short locator table, no quotes: each row gives a claim, its marker, the document and the page, with any column your authority requires there. A marker that names no citation refuses the answer."; D106's unverified sentence stays. `instruction.md` lists the refusal: "A marker `[C<n>]` in the Markdown names the citation at place n of `citations`; one that names no citation is refused." `forecast_extension.md`'s "Preserve source-supplied JSON-pointer assignments (/path = JSON value) verbatim in the handoff and cite the complete assignment quotes." (there for `carries`) is now "Cite each source-supplied JSON-pointer assignment (/path = JSON value) you use as the complete assignment line, and put its marker beside the value it supports." **The vendor's Evidence Trace contract asks for no quoted text:** the canon's envelope requires the `## Evidence Trace` heading and leaves its content to the module (its template body is `...`); its evidence field is "Source title, period, page/section/line if available" (`CANON_SHARED.md`); CP-0's T6 "Evidence Trace" register lists "evidence ID, original source, active representation and locator" (`CP-0_SCHEMA_REFERENCE.md`; the payload schema's `evidence_trace` has the same four fields, and SKILL.md sets T6's columns "none"); CP-6 asks "claim → evidence, with lineage_class", CP-4C "claim, legal, valuation and formula lineage", CP-3D "quote and calculation lineage" (market price quotes). So the prompt keeps the heading, asks for claim, marker, document and page, and keeps "any column your authority requires there". CP-2H's SKILL.md asks that each agency trigger be cited "by quoting, verbatim, the complete evidence line that states it", while "the registers and prose may paraphrase the trigger with that locator": met by the citation's quote with a marker in the prose; no vendor file is edited. **Replay** (`scratchpad/mk1/replay.py`, read-only, NB1's replay over the 25 current-schema run databases, the stage of an older build's `HANDOFF_MALFORMED` read by the 99a2fb4 body check): all 134 stored answers get the same verdict as under D106 (43 accepted answers re-accept, E1 CP-0 #2 with its one unverified citation, C3 CP-0 #1 still meets F497; 44 refused attempts accept), and no stored answer names a missing citation. Old answers have no markers, so of the 1,649 citations re-judged, 2 are linked by a marker: C2 CP-1 #1 (31 citations) wrote `[C1]`, `[C2]` and `[C1, C2]` as its own row tags in a table, beside `[G1]` and `[G3]`; of the 87 answers now accepted, 1,340 of 1,342 anchored citations are unlinked. All 44 accepted records re-encode to their own bytes, hold no `marker`, and re-anchor unchanged through the new `_reanchored`; their 722 citations keep `linked` true as recorded. Evidence: `tests/test_citation_markers.py`, `tests/test_handoff_record.py::test_a_citation_no_marker_names_is_not_linked`, `tests/test_second_attempt.py::test_a_marker_naming_no_citation_gets_a_retry_naming_it`, `::test_retry_feedback_reports_the_vendors_message_and_the_quote_count`, `tests/test_nonblocking_citations.py::test_the_partition_keeps_order_codes_and_links`, `::test_each_citation_fault_keeps_the_answer`, `::test_the_retry_lines_on_citations_say_what_they_are`, `tests/test_canonical_execution.py::test_one_unanchorable_quote_is_kept_unverified_beside_the_anchored_one`, `tests/test_forecast_route.py::test_a_calculation_input_that_does_not_anchor_gets_a_guided_retry` (`restated`, `unmarked`), `::test_the_binder_takes_an_owner_citation_a_marker_names`, `tests/test_upstream_citation_register.py::test_quote_existence_is_host_verified_support_is_left_to_cp5`, `tests/test_handoff_invocation.py::test_the_prompt_repeats_the_closed_contract_after_evidence`. **Fix round 1** (the adversarial audit of MK1, probes in `scratchpad/audit-mk1/`). *A marker in a figure cell wedged CP-CF (Critical):* "Put a marker beside each material figure" invited `| (45) [C1] |`; the vendor accepted CP-2G's cell and CP-CF's `driver_value` could never map it, so the run stopped at 9 of 10 (the vendor's own `cp_tables.parse_figure("2993 [C3]")` raises too). The prompt now says "A marker never goes inside a figure, status or other value cell: put it in the row's source or evidence column if it has one, otherwise in the prose or the Evidence Trace row." (`forecast_extension.md` likewise: "its marker in the row's source column or the Evidence Trace, never in a value cell"), and every host reader of a value cell reads it without its markers (`citation_markers.unmarked`, pure, removing each marker, escaped or not, and the spaces before it, and nothing else, `(45) [C1]` → `(45)`): CP-2G's driver rows (`forecast._faults`), a table cell's figure (`tables.figure_value`, which hands the vendor's `parse_figure` the unmarked cell), a qualification key's cell (`matrix._normalised_cell`) and a T8 demand cell (`selection.demand_items`). No vendor parser is edited, so a vendor validator that refuses a marked cell (CP-0's T8 status) stays a structural refusal with a guided retry, and the prompt covers it. The audit's full-route probe now completes 10 of 10 with `(45) [C1]`. *An upstream or self-made `[C<n>]` (Important):* the final check reserves `[C<n>]` for this answer's citations ("label your own conflicts and rows otherwise (for example `CF-1`)") and says "An upstream handoff's marker is shown as `[CP-1 C3]`: keep that form if you carry it over, never `[C3]`, and cite the excerpt yourself to rest a claim of yours on it." A downstream prompt shows each upstream body with its markers qualified by module (`citation_markers.qualified`, pure over the bytes: `[C3]` in CP-1's body is `[CP-1 C3]`, `[C2, C5]` is `[CP-1 C2, C5]`); the stored handoff, its digest and the section's `sha256` are unchanged, and the section header says so. The register line now opens `marker: CP-1 C3`, and its header no longer calls every line a located quote, so it stays true of the marked unverified lines MK2 adds: "each line names a citation of an accepted upstream handoff by its qualified marker, CP-1 C3 for the [CP-1 C3] by which that handoff cites it above; a located one carries the quote the host found word for word in the evidence delivered to that module when it was accepted, an unverified one says so and carries none" (the instruction's "register of their located citations" is now "of their citations", `icm/CONTEXT.md` likewise). A model that copies `[CP-1 C3]` writes plain text: no link, no dangling refusal, no near-miss hint. CP-3's SKILL.md ("Inherit the upstream Definition Conflict Register verbatim … with original source citations") now sees each inherited row's citation as `[CP-1 C3]`, which it carries as is and the register resolves; that reads as intended. *Minors:* the CP-CF stage reference (`icm/stages/cp-cf/references/SKILL.md`) no longer asks for the binding quotes in the handoff: the bindings carry them, each is cited again with its `[C<n>]` in the Evidence Trace, and no other source text is quoted. Linked is nearly anchored: with Evidence Trace a locator table holding a marker per citation, almost every anchored citation will be linked, so "Linked only" scoring now excludes little beyond a citation the model never placed; accepted as the owner's ruling reads. The guard now judges by quote, as the binder does (`canonical._unbindable`, `_calculation_line`): a quote binds when some anchored citation of it is marker-linked, so an unmarked or unverified second citation of a bound quote is no fault. A marker of ten or more digits names no citation (`MARKER_DIGITS` 9; `[C0000000001]` is dangling), `[C01]` names citation 1, and the dangling line shows each marker as written (`[C02]`, cut past 12 digits). `instruction.md` drops "no other rule is stated". Two surviving mutants are killed (a list with two spaces after its comma is not a marker; a marker on a record under an earlier rule does not write). Evidence: `tests/test_citation_markers.py::test_unmarked_reads_a_cell_without_its_markers_and_moves_nothing_else`, `::test_an_upstream_body_is_shown_with_its_markers_qualified`, `::test_the_dangling_line_shows_each_marker_as_written`, `::test_markers_belong_only_to_an_excerpt_record`, `tests/test_forecast_route.py::test_cp_cf_maps_each_cp2g_sign_to_the_calculator_end_to_end[marked-marked]`, `::test_the_guard_judges_by_quote_as_the_binder_does`, `tests/test_upstream_citation_register.py::test_mandatory_registers_and_disclosed_conflicts_reach_consumers_unchanged`. **Fix round 2** (the re-review, `scratchpad/audit-mk1/p_fix.py`): `qualified` rewrote the upstream's front matter too, which `markers` never reads, so a carried `validation_warnings` holding `[C3]` was shown as `[CP-0 C3]` and `verify_owner_restrictions` (exact comparison, at CP-CF and the CP-5 screen) would refuse a consumer that copied it as shown, `HANDOFF_INCOMPLETE`, with no retry able to fix it; and it missed an escaped comma (`[C1\, C2]`), which `markers` reads, leaving a live marker downstream. `citation_markers` now holds the one reading, `body` (after the front matter, escapes read; `handoff` reads markers through it), and `qualified` rewrites exactly the spans `body` reads as markers, mapped back through the escapes, and leaves the front matter and every other byte as stored (a 20,000-sample fuzz finds no live marker after it). `unmarked` no longer joins a figure a marker splits: a marker with figure marks (`0-9`, `.`, `,`) on both sides stands as ` | `, which no figure reader takes (`4[C1]5`, `1,2[C1]50` and `(4[C1]5)` read as no figure, where they read 45, 1,250 and -45); the audit's 19 other cells read as before. No golden moves. Evidence: `tests/test_citation_markers.py::test_a_marker_that_splits_a_figure_leaves_no_figure`, `::test_qualified_rewrites_exactly_the_markers_the_body_reads`.

- F506 (2026-10-04, Task MK2 of `markers-plan.md`; D107) — D107's display: markers are chips, citation lists are compact, the full line is shown in the source drawers alone (and in a saved artifact's signed record, opt-in, fix round 1), and the deliverable lists each module's citations in a compact appendix. **Owner, verbatim:** "the app should not have large bodies of citation text buried with analysis. The user can open the source documents to view these.", and "Compact, source on click (Recommended)": "Show document · page · the short excerpt. Clicking opens the source with the full line highlighted, so a dropped qualifier is still one click away. The deliverable lists citations compactly in an appendix rather than inline full lines." **Merge.** The tree MK1 merged into (0962cf9 + c4f3b16, b38f6cc) was green as it stood: the full suite and the 572 workspace tests passed, NB2's display reading `linked` whatever it means; nothing was fixed for the merge. **Wire** (`caos/api/wire.py`): `CitationView`, `UnverifiedCitationView`, `BlockedQuoteView`, `NarrativeFigure` and `NarrativeUnverified` gain `marker` (`Marker`, null before D107), from the record; `ReportArtifact` gains `figures` and `unverified`, each marked record citation as a narrative figure carries it and each marked unverified one, so a saved artifact's chip opens the drawer (`reports._marked`; the Report and Committee reads resolve those documents with the narrative's in their one `cited_source_ids` query). Schema regenerated; `documents.ts` validates the fields closed. **Chips** (`frontend/src/evidence/Markers.tsx`, `ds/markdown.ts` `readMarkers`, `ds/ModelMarkdown.tsx` `CitationMarkerLink`): wherever a module's Markdown is drawn -- the Analysis module views (lead, parts, registers, appendix, audit; `HandoffMarkers`) and a saved artifact's Formatted view in Report and Committee (`ArtifactMarkers`) -- `[C<n>]` and `[C2, C5]` are read as `handoff._MARKER` reads them (a backslash escape is the mark it writes; `[c3]`, `[C 3]`, `[C3-C5]`, a Unicode digit stay text). An anchored citation's chip is a button `C<n>`, named "citation n: <file>, page N", that opens the source drawer at its page, where its whole line is shown with the excerpt marked; an unverified one's is a dashed span, "C<n> · unverified – page N" ("citation n" to a screen reader), no control, opening nothing; a number no citation holds, a record from before markers, or a page with no markers in scope (the filed HTML) leaves the bracket as written. React escapes every character. **Compact lists** (`frontend/src/evidence/compact.ts` `clampExcerpt`: whitespace runs single, past 120 code points cut with an ellipsis): Analysis's source facts (the chip shows `C<n>`), the Audit tab's unverified citations (until now the model's quote in full), the Blocked view (its twelve-word cut becomes the same clamp), a saved narrative's figures (the chip names `CP-1 C3 · p.N`), the report picker and draft list (`figures.choiceText`: `C3 · excerpt: …`; F504's `wordPlace` goes with the line). `TracedLine` is used by the two drawers alone. **Deliverable** (`caos/deliverable/render.py`): a module is its analysis (markers kept as text), the host's calculations, then "Citations": one line per citation in the answer's list order where the record keeps markers -- `[C1] · <document> · page 4 · “excerpt” · verified`, `[C2] · unverified – page 7 · source <id> · the model's quote “…” · claim lineage: Untraced · not located` -- with " · quote (source line not recorded)" on an `ANY_RUN` quote and " · not linked to a statement in the answer" where no marker names it. No line and no `<mark>` anywhere on the page; a narrative figure is its clamped excerpt and `[CP-1 C3] · <document> · page N`, an unverified one its label first. `clamped` cuts on the workspace's own whitespace set, so both show the same text. A marker that is no place refuses `DELIVERABLE_PAYLOAD_INVALID`. §45.6's order becomes the model's, the host's calculations, then what the host located, never mixed. This is a deliberate departure from the legacy `render` parity group: the 13 rendering cases move (the same 13 F504 moved); only that group was regenerated, from the legacy snapshot with the appendix mirrored (D34's procedure, `scratchpad/mk2/mirror_render.py` over NB2's mirror, the snapshot first reproducing the committed goldens byte for byte; mirrored legacy and host agree on all 30 cases); `RENDERER_SHA256` re-pinned. **Register** (MK1's note): a marker that names an unverified upstream citation has its own line in its place, `- marker: CP-0 C2 unverified – page 7: the host did not locate this citation's quote (CITATION_AMBIGUOUS), so none is listed here; quote_existence: NOT_VERIFIED_BY_HOST support: NOT_ASSESSED_BY_HOST (CP-5 audit)` (`invocation._unverified_line`, `build_handoff_prompt(upstream_unverified=…)`, `canonical._Context.unverified`); no quote is listed, and an unverified citation no marker names is still not. No `prompt` golden moves. **a11y:** the gate scans an anchored chip in Analysis (CP-0, added), an unverified one (CP-1B) and both kinds in Report's and Committee's artifacts, both schemes: 840 entries, 0 violations, 0 layout failures. Evidence: `tests/test_citation_markers_served.py`, `tests/test_citations_appendix.py`, `tests/test_deliverable_render.py::test_an_excerpt_is_listed_compactly_never_inside_its_whole_line`, `::test_a_narrative_figure_is_its_excerpt_and_a_reference_never_its_line`, `::test_a_record_without_a_line_lists_its_quote_labelled`, `tests/test_unverified_citations_shown.py`, `tests/test_upstream_citation_register.py::test_a_marker_naming_an_unverified_citation_gets_a_line_without_a_quote`, `::test_the_register_reaches_a_consumer_with_its_upstreams_unverified_marker`; `frontend/tests/unit/markers.test.tsx`, `evidence-page.test.tsx` `test_a_marker_chip_in_the_analysis_opens_the_source_drawer_at_its_line`, `test_an_artifacts_marker_chip_opens_its_citations_source_page`, `report-figures.test.tsx` `test_the_picker_names_each_citation_compactly_by_its_marker_and_excerpt`, `test_a_long_quote_is_clamped_to_about_one_line`, `unverified.test.tsx`, `analysis.test.tsx`. **Fix round 1** (the MK2 audit, probes in `scratchpad/audit-mk2/`; MK1's fix round merged first, 553278e: its register names markers qualified, so an unverified line reads `marker: CP-0 C2 unverified – page N: …`, under MK1's header, which covers it; no `prompt` golden moves). *Important, full lines beside the analysis:* Report and Committee showed each artifact's signed record JSON, every `line_text` in it, under the artifact; it is now a native `<details>` "Signed record (JSON)", closed by default, its summary a keyboard control (`ds/ArtifactMarkdown.tsx`). Full source lines are shown in the source drawers and in that opt-in record, nowhere else. *Important, a dead register link:* `[CP-1 C3]`, an upstream citation as a downstream prompt names it (`citation_markers.qualified`), was read by `readRefs` as CP-1's register C3; `REF_REGISTER` no longer takes `C<n>`, and a qualified marker is an inert label "CP-1 C3" (`QUALIFIED`, `.qmark`); no chip opens the upstream citation, since a body may name a module the page does not hold. *Important, chip mapping untested:* C2 and C3 are pressed in a module's text and in a saved artifact and open their own citations; the `index: 0` and `index + 1` mutants of both die. *Minors:* the page reads markers exactly as `handoff.markers` does -- no marker across a line break (paragraphs and quotes keep the written breaks, shown as spaces), escapes in it read (`[C3\, C4]`), any digits with `MARKER_DIGITS` -- held by one shared case file, `frontend/tests/unit/marker-cases.json`, on both sides (`tests/test_citation_marker_cases.py`, `markers.test.tsx`), fenced code, comments, a `--- ` block and an unformattable table drawn as written and saying so; the clamp is exactly 120 code points on both sides, the two constants held equal, and the deliverable cuts before it escapes (pinned by behaviour); each chip's accessible name starts with its visible text (WCAG 2.5.3), the Blocked chip's by its marker; a narrative figure's chip reads "CP-1 C3 · p.7", `NarrativeFigure` and `NarrativeUnverified` carrying `module_id`.
